# Path Mobile: offline transfer through copy/paste, Windows PowerShell 5.1+.
# Source app commit: d8763ec28690c956c4b2abcc8189c051f26688b6
# The Base64 argument below is ZIP DATA, not encoded PowerShell commands.
# This block verifies SHA-256, creates a NEW test folder under your user profile,
# extracts the preview and changes into its app folder. It makes no network
# requests, installs nothing, changes no execution policy, and deletes nothing.
# Copy the WHOLE file into PowerShell, then press Enter.
& {
    param([string] $PathMobilePreviewZipBase64)
    try {
        $PathMobilePreviewBytes = [Convert]::FromBase64String($PathMobilePreviewZipBase64)
        $PathMobilePreviewHasher = [System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
        try {
            $PathMobilePreviewActualHash = [BitConverter]::ToString($PathMobilePreviewHasher.ComputeHash($PathMobilePreviewBytes)).Replace('-', '').ToLowerInvariant()
        } finally {
            $PathMobilePreviewHasher.Dispose()
        }
        if ($PathMobilePreviewActualHash -ne '6863bf415ab2a53db263e6bf47844641e1134f3d006457e78eb333dad51819ea') {
            throw 'Archive checksum mismatch. Copy the entire text file again; nothing has been written.'
        }
        $PathMobilePreviewRoot = Join-Path -Path $env:USERPROFILE -ChildPath 'PathMobile-Test-d8763ec'
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PathMobilePreviewRoot) {
            $PathMobilePreviewRoot = $PathMobilePreviewRoot + '-' + [Guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N').Substring(0, 8)
        }
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $PathMobilePreviewRoot -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
        $PathMobilePreviewZipPath = Join-Path -Path $PathMobilePreviewRoot -ChildPath 'path-mobile-alpha-windows-preview.zip'
        [System.IO.File]::WriteAllBytes($PathMobilePreviewZipPath, $PathMobilePreviewBytes)
        Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $PathMobilePreviewZipPath -DestinationPath $PathMobilePreviewRoot -ErrorAction Stop
        $PathMobilePreviewAppRoot = Join-Path -Path $PathMobilePreviewRoot -ChildPath 'Path-Mobile'
        if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path -Path $PathMobilePreviewAppRoot -ChildPath 'index.html') -PathType Leaf)) {
            throw 'The expected app folder is missing. Keep the ZIP for inspection.'
        }
        Set-Location -LiteralPath $PathMobilePreviewAppRoot -ErrorAction Stop
        Write-Host ''
        Write-Host 'PATH MOBILE READY'
        Write-Host ('Folder: ' + $PathMobilePreviewAppRoot)
        Write-Host 'Run next: py -3 -m http.server 8010 --bind 127.0.0.1'
        Write-Host 'Then open: http://127.0.0.1:8010'
        Write-Host 'Keep this window open while the server is running. Ctrl+C stops it.'
    } catch {
        Write-Error -Message ('Path Mobile restore failed: ' + $_.Exception.Message)
    }
} @'
